Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:2:name: Port-note discipline — cite stability across port-note insertions
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:3:description: When porting a file with a port-note header, line-number cites pointing INTO the ported file silently drift by N lines (where N = port-note length). FORWARD-REF labels go stale when targets land. Inherited upstream cite errors propagate. n-counter claims undercount actual occurrences. This rule codifies the 4 disciplines that prevent these drift classes.
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:7:# Port-note discipline — cite stability across port-note insertions
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:9:# TIER-3 local codification provenance only: this rule is design-novel for claude-sota (no Z:/claude port source). It codifies 4 cumulative arc patterns surfaced by retrospective T3 audit on 7 commits (37e1710..ba8c6e6) — Pattern P1 port-note 3-line offset propagation (n=4), Pattern P2 FORWARD-REF staleness on downstream materialization (n=2), Pattern P3 inherited upstream cite errors (n=1 strong), Pattern P4 n-counter undercounts (n=2). Verdict file `.claude/state/codex_consult_p4meta_port_note_discipline_OUT.txt` is an evidence trail, not SOTA authority.
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:11:A port commit that adds a port-note header of N lines silently shifts every line below it by +N. Cites pointing INTO the ported file from sibling files OR self-cites within the port-note itself use UPSTREAM coordinates by reflex; no compile error fires; readers click and land in the wrong place. n=4 evidence across iter-10/12/13 of this fire's port arc demonstrates the drift class is structural, not coincidental.
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:26:When adding a port-note header of N lines to a ported file, cites pointing INTO that file from anywhere (sibling files, the port-note's own self-cites, README/CLAUDE.md/rules, commit bodies) carry UPSTREAM coordinates by default. Compensate.
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:35:2. **Line-bump compensation (fallback)**: when the port edit is a **pure header insertion before the cited span** (no other line additions/deletions), bump every cite by +N where N = port-note line count. Run before commit:
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:37:   N=<port-note line count>
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:41:   ⚠️ **Caveat**: line-bump is INVALID when the port edit involved any other content addition/deletion below the port-note. In that case, fall back to symbol-anchor or resolve-by-content (`grep <symbol> <ported_file>` to find new line, then update cite).
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:47:Rules + port-notes routinely write `FORWARD-REF — <X> pending P<N> port` for things not yet shipped. When P<N> ships, every such cite must be rewritten to drop the FORWARD-REF qualifier (or escalate to a stronger status — but see §6 anti-pattern about `ACTIVE`).
Z:\claude-sota\.claude\rules\port-note-discipline.md:77:**Discipline**: every `n=N evidence` claim in commit body / port-note / rule file MUST be backed by an explicit query + scope record:

across rounds. Pattern A collapses the iter chain into 2 steps (T1 + apply).

**Pattern B** prevents the false-failure anti-pattern: without explicit
trace-mining discipline, T1 timeouts feel like loop blockers and tempt
re-firing T1 with broader prompts (which makes timeouts MORE likely).
Pattern B reframes timeout as a known-handled mode where verification
shifts from T1 → T3 + prior-fire research, preserving cross-model consensus
without latency tax.

**Together**: across 8 ships in 2 /loop fires (iter-22→iter-26 + security
commit), 5 followed Pattern A and 3 followed Pattern B. All 8 produced
clean atomic commits. Median wall-time per ship ≈ 12-18 min including T1
fire-and-poll. Pre-pattern era: 3-5 iters × ~30 min = 90-150 min per
converged ship.

## When NOT to apply

- **Trivial typo fixes** (≤5 LOC delta, single-correctness change, no NEW cite class introduced, no NEW table row, no NEW rule body section, no semantic change to load-bearing claim) — skip T1. Tightened iter-N+6 per code-reviewer iter-25.1 P2-4 finding: iter-25 commit `544824c` rationalized 54-LOC SKILL.md addition with NEW Q2-2026 TIER-1 cite class as "trivial doc" — incorrect. Trivial = surgical-correctness fix (rename / cite-range correction / typo / inverted-count); NOT additive-new-content even if formally documentation. Apply T1 SYSTEM-meta-review fallback per `codex-t1-system-meta-review-fallback.md` if a prior verdict prescribes the change; otherwise fire fresh T1.
- **Bash-only operational commands** (no design surface to review) — skip T1.
- **Pattern A timeout escalation**: if codex T1 returns conf <0.85 OR REJECT,
  do NOT apply prescriptions blindly. Escalate to user per cross-model-consensus.md
  HARD GATE.
- **Pattern B trace contains red-flags** (not just empty/sandbox-blocked):
  if codex started writing critical findings but timed out mid-finding,
  treat as Pattern A NEEDS-REVISION partial — re-fire T1 with finding excerpt
  pinned in prompt to land structured verdict.
- **Codex 0.125+ schema-format change**: if codex's JSON output schema changes
  (currently `verdict ∈ {approve, needs-attention}` per vendored
  `.claude/schemas/review-output.schema.json`), update parsing in BOTH
  consumer hooks (`codex_postcommit_review.py`, `codex_prepush_review.py`,
  `codex_stop_review_gate.py`) BEFORE relying on this rule.

## Pattern-B mitigation patterns (gstack-derived; n=8 codification — Loop #4 Fire 9 + Wave 16 fire-9 increment)

Three portable patterns from `Z:/repos/deps/gstack/codex/SKILL.md @ HEAD dde55103fcc42bd446d804ddc15567ced8455ac1` Step 2B Challenge mode (MIT license, Copyright (c) 2026 Garry Tan) directly address the **zero-investigation Pattern B variant** observed n=8 times across Loop #4 fires + Wave 16 cron fire-9 (where codex CLI exits 0 but produces 50-60 line prompt-echo with no model response, no tool calls, no `turn.completed` events). These are METHODOLOGY DOCUMENTATION; OPERATIONAL adoption (refactoring `codex_t1_consult_gate.py` and sibling hooks to use `--json` + parse `turn.completed`) is queued as a separate fire per kiss-dry-yagni minimum-LOC.

| Pattern | gstack cite | What it mitigates |
|---|---|---|
| **`turn.completed` event-count completeness check** | `gstack/codex/SKILL.md:1046-1048` — `if turn_completed_count == 0: print('[codex warning] No turn.completed event received — possible mid-stream disconnect.', file=sys.stderr)` | Distinguishes "completed with no findings" from "mid-stream disconnect / zero-investigation Pattern B"; ZERO `turn.completed` events = positive signal of disconnect |
| **Hang detection on exit 124** | `gstack/codex/SKILL.md:1051-1056` — `if [ "$_CODEX_EXIT" = "124" ]; then ... echo "Codex stalled past 10 minutes."` | Distinguishes "timed out" (exit 124) from "completed cleanly" (exit 0); current sss hooks treat both as exit 0 since `&` background-task exit code is the wrapper's, not codex's |
| **Adversarial framing pattern** | `gstack/codex/SKILL.md:991-994,1003` — "Be adversarial. Be thorough. No compliments — just the problems." | sss current consult prompts use "audit" framing which may produce more polite reviews missing aggressive findings; explicit attacker-mindset framing is methodology-quality lift orthogonal to Pattern B mitigation |

## §6 Anti-patterns

- **"Verbatim port preserves correctness"** — refuted by Pattern P3. Verbatim port preserves CONTENT but not CORRECTNESS-OF-CITES; inherited cite errors propagate forever otherwise. Cite-class verbatim verify (§4) is the recursive check.
- **"Cite-class-only audit (file existence)"** — refuted by Pattern P3. Codex T1 saw FORWARD-REF status without content verify. Add §4 discipline to T1 prompt template.
- **"Bump line numbers blindly"** — refuted by §2 Discipline 1 caveat. Line-bump compensation is INVALID when port edit involves any content addition/deletion beyond the port-note. Symbol-anchor is the safer SOTA pattern.
- **"FORWARD-REF retirement is hook-driven; ship it now"** — partially refuted by current `.claude/settings.json` state. Hook stack registers PreToolUse Edit|Write|MultiEdit for T1 and PostToolUse Bash(git commit *) for postcommit review/audits, but still lacks a dedicated T3 retirement-grep consumer [VERIFIED 2026-04-28 via `.claude/settings.json:273-335`]. T1 enforcement and generic postcommit review are hook-driven; FORWARD-REF retirement remains iter-end manual grep per §3 until a dedicated consumer lands.
- **"Script-landed = ACTIVE"** — CATEGORY-CLAIM drift per `.claude/rules/synthesis-layer-verify.md` §Subclaim-type discriminator. A hook script existing at `.claude/hooks/scripts/X.py` does NOT mean it fires at runtime. Verify wire status by reading `.claude/settings.json` BEFORE flipping any rule's lifecycle table to ACTIVE. Honest intermediate states: `script-landed/unwired`, `script-landed/wired-pending-codex-CLI-install`, `script-landed/wired/disabled-by-env`. Use them.
- **"n-of-mind counting"** — refuted by Pattern P4. Run actual `grep -c` before promoting; record the query in the n-claim itself.
- **"Rewrite historical commit bodies to fix retroactive findings"** — refuted by codex iter-16 AXIS-2 prescription "Do not rewrite the iter-12 historical commit body as part of normal fix-forward." Historical commits are the audit trail; fix the LIVE state, codify the discipline forward.

## §7 Sibling-rule integration

- `.claude/rules/synthesis-layer-verify.md` — §Reporting categories OVER/UNDER/HONEST-NON-FINDING applies recursively to codex T1 output (§4 Discipline 3). Subclaim-type discriminator UPSTREAM/OPERATIONAL/CATEGORY surfaces "script-landed = ACTIVE" as CATEGORY-CLAIM drift (§6 anti-pattern).
- `.claude/rules/cross-model-consensus.md` — §T1-T6 lifecycle table is ITSELF subject to §3 FORWARD-REF retirement discipline. Lifecycle status flips MUST verify wire-state per §6 anti-pattern.
- `.claude/rules/audit-action-loop.md` — §4-stage Wire / Surface / Close / Re-fire pattern is the mechanization analogue of §3 FORWARD-REF retirement. When FUTURE-AUTOMATION lands (postcommit hook wiring), this rule's §3 discipline becomes audit-action-loop's "Surface" stage.
- `.claude/rules/evidence-policy.md` — n-counter claims (§5) require `[VERIFIED via <query>]` markers per evidence-policy. Bare n-counter claims without query attribution fail the marker discipline.
- `.claude/rules/sota-pin-discipline.md` — Tier-1/Tier-3 cited-repo HEAD SHAs in PINS.json are subject to §4 cite-class verbatim verify when the parenthetical claim about content drifts on upstream changes.

# SOTA-installed manifest — single source of truth for claude-sota-installed

> **Purpose**: every architectural primitive in `claude-sota-installed` is tracked here with: install or cite-import command + upstream HEAD SHA + axis 1+2+3 verdict + Probe DAG outcome + smoke-probe verification + disposition. Per cardinal-rule-5 install-priority + cardinal-rule-6 fresh-from-github + cardinal-rule "zero self-inventing" (user directive 2026-05-06).
>
> **Two install classes** (per user directive 2026-05-06 "all the sources form advanced repos, and guide"):
>
> - **INSTALL-CLASS** — code/binary that RUNS in the runtime (plugin / MCP server / CLI binary / direct .claude/* import / hook script). Lives under `.claude/plugins/cache/` / `.mcp.json` / system PATH / `.claude/{rules,agents,skills,commands,hooks/scripts}/`.
> - **CITE-CLASS** — documentation/guide that authorities a primitive without running. Cited at `<repo>/<file>:<line> @ HEAD <SHA>` in this manifest's "Source guide" column. Examples: CCBP `claude-settings.md`, Karpathy gist, Boris Cherny tips.
>
> Every row carries BOTH classes where applicable (the install + the guide it implements).

## Status legend

- `INSTALLED` — install complete + smoke-probe PASS + axis-1+2+3 verified
- `INSTALLED-VIA-SYSTEM-PATH` — primitive available via system PATH (winget / chocolatey / package-manager-system-scope) at acceptable freshness per cardinal-rule-6; version anchor + smoke probe + axis verdict captured in install-provenance.md (added Wave 50 fire 4 per Agent C-redo P1-3 — fire 3 PATH-discovery introduced this disposition)
- `INSTALLED-AMBER` — install complete but Probe DAG flagged P2/P3 issue; manifest carries the deferred fix
- `CITE-IMPORT-AMBER` — sibling cite-import per Section 14 / 14.5 exception; subagent or hook copied with sibling commit-SHA cite trail; re-evaluate when sibling commit-SHA churns >50% post-import
- `STAGED` — install command run but smoke-probe pending
- `DEFERRED-PENDING-FIX` — install blocked on upstream issue (e.g., breaking change, license incompat); cite-import permitted as substitute pending fix
- `REJECTED-POST-PROBE` — Probe DAG returned REJECT-FOR-FIT or axis-fail; cite-only retained for forward research
- `PLANNED` — design phase; install command drafted but not yet run
- `CITE-ONLY` — guide source cited as authority; no install needed (e.g., CCBP cardinal-rule cites)

### Status legend extension — Wave 146 Fire 3 refinement (W146-F3 method-extension, NOT Agent A verbatim)

**Per W146-F3 codex T1 NEEDS-REVISION conf=0.91 MODIFY #1** [VERIFIED via `.claude/state/codex_consult_w146_f3_p1_manifest_hygiene_OUT.txt` JSON EOF]: this section EXTENDS the legend above with 2 NEW distinguishing classes (`INSTALLED-ACTIVE` / `INSTALLED-DORMANT`) per Agent C UNDER-claim #2 from Wave 146 Fire 1, AND codifies reproducible SOTA-cleanliness scoring weights. Existing legend entries `INSTALLED-AMBER` (L17) + `CITE-IMPORT-AMBER` (L18) remain DISTINCT per codex W146-F3 finding (they are NOT the same bucket; INSTALLED-AMBER = installed primitive with deferred P2/P3 risk, CITE-IMPORT-AMBER = sibling/local-composition provenance under Section 14.5 with effective_tier=TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION).

**Cite class**: `constituents=[TIER-1-DIRECT @ codex W146-F3 verdict file EOF + W146-F1 Agent A weighted-method, TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION @ W146-F3 method-refinement]; effective_tier=TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION` per `Z:/claude-sota/.claude/rules/citation-discipline.md` rule #8 MIN_PRECEDENCE.

- `INSTALLED-ACTIVE` (NEW W146-F3) — Installed via official native channel + smoke PASS AND actively WIRED in `.claude/settings.json` hook chain OR `.mcp.json` MCP server OR `.codex/config.toml` profile. Distinct from bare `INSTALLED` to surface wiring-vs-presence
- `INSTALLED-DORMANT` (NEW W146-F3) — Files present + SHA-pinned BUT NOT wired into active runtime. Materially cleaner than `PLANNED` per Agent C UNDER-claim #2 (cwc primitives example) but NOT runtime-clean. Re-class to `INSTALLED-ACTIVE` upon wire-activation

### SOTA-cleanliness scoring (W146-F3 method-refinement of W146-F1 Agent A baseline 61%)

Per W146-F1 Agent A baseline (61% overall / band 55-65% per Agent C calibration) + W146-F3 method-refinement: future cleanliness % audits MUST disclose (a) total rows counted, (b) rows excluded (HISTORICAL-MACHINE-EXCLUDED markers + REJECTED-POST-PROBE), (c) per-class counts, (d) computed weighted %, (e) cite-class chain. **Reproducibility predicate**: same input manifest + same method → same output %. **Method-change disclosure**: Agent A treated AMBER/PARTIAL/DEFERRED/REJECTED as 0 clean (verbatim); W146-F3 introduces fractional +0.5 weights for INSTALLED-DORMANT/INSTALLED-AMBER/INSTALLED-PARTIAL — disclose as W146-F3 refinement when re-computing % under this method, NOT Agent A verbatim.

| Class | Cleanliness weight (W146-F3) | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| `INSTALLED` / `INSTALLED-VIA-SYSTEM-PATH` / `INSTALLED-ACTIVE` | +1.0 | Fully clean |
| `INSTALLED-DORMANT` (NEW W146-F3) | +0.5 | Cleaner than PLANNED; deferred to wire-activation |
| `INSTALLED-AMBER` | +0.5 (W146-F3) / 0 (Agent A verbatim) | Installed primitive with deferred P2/P3 — disclose method |
| `INSTALLED-PARTIAL` | +0.5 (W146-F3) / 0 (Agent A verbatim) | Upstream substrate exists but wiring contract incomplete |
| `CITE-IMPORT-AMBER` | 0 | Distinct from INSTALLED-AMBER per codex W146-F3; sibling cite-import per Section 14.5; effective_tier=TIER-3-LOCAL-COMPOSITION; non-clean until upstream-parity-promoted |
| `STAGED` / `DEFERRED-PENDING-FIX` / `PLANNED` | 0 | Identified but not installed |
| `CITE-ONLY` | +1.0 (different denominator class) | Research-only; no install artifact |
| `REJECTED-POST-PROBE` / `HISTORICAL-MACHINE-EXCLUDED` | excluded | Not counted in denominator |
